1998 
I received the attached email from Dr. Vinod K. Bhatt
(vinodkbhatt at hotmail.com) of Uttar Pradesh, India.
He is interested in having his CV circulated in
North America with the view to learning of visiting
researcher positions in Agaricology.  Persons
wishing to contact Dr. Bhatt should do so directly.
For those who may have difficulty extracting
the CV automatically, the attachment is
a uuencoded Pagemaker 5 file that was concatenated
with Dr. Bhatt's mail and is at the end of this
message.

Sincerely,

Rod Tulloss

Dear Dr. Tulloss

Got your message today.
I also got mail from Dr. Yang, he certainly will be a valuable
correspondent.
Please find my CV as attach file,
Rest soon,
Thanking you with good wishes and regards
Sincerely

Vinod
